<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.salon.com/2026/11/05/goodbye-from-salon/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Goodbye from Salon</span><span class='zh'>Salon 告别</span></a><span class="dom">salon.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Salon has announced that it will cease publication after 31 years.</span><span class='zh'>Salon 宣布，在出版 31 年后将停止运营。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Salon has announced that it will cease publication after 31 years. The publication described its history as an attempt to explore what journalism could become while the internet was still new.</span><span class='zh'>Salon 宣布，在出版 31 年后将停止运营。该出版物将自己的历史描述为一次探索新闻业可能形态的尝试，起步于互联网仍处于早期阶段之时。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>4.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>557</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50383739' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>212</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50331402" data-id="50331402" data-tags="Open Source" data-score="531" data-comments="317" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50331402" data-time="1793578647">
<a class="thumb" href="https://occupysaas.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://occupysaas.com/assets/img/og.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='occupysaas.com' data-c='#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">38</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://occupysaas.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OccupySaaS</span><span class='zh'>OccupySaaS</span></a><span class="dom">occupysaas.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OccupySaaS is an Apache-2.0 open-source effort to build self-hosted, drop-in replacements for major SaaS products without per-seat fees.</span><span class='zh'>OccupySaaS 是一个采用 Apache-2.0 许可的开源项目，旨在构建可自托管、无需按席位收费的大型 SaaS 替代品。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OccupySaaS is an Apache-2.0 open-source effort to build self-hosted, drop-in replacements for major SaaS products without per-seat fees. Its first projects are ZeroAuthy, positioned as an Auth0 replacement, and SalesCompel, positioned as a Salesforce replacement.</span><span class='zh'>OccupySaaS 是一个采用 Apache-2.0 许可的开源项目，旨在构建可自托管、无需按席位收费的大型 SaaS 替代品。它的首批项目包括定位为 Auth0 替代品的 ZeroAuthy，以及定位为 Salesforce 替代品的 SalesCompel。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>531</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50331402' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>317</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50336918" data-id="50336918" data-tags="AI|Hardware" data-score="529" data-comments="228" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50336918" data-time="1793628162">
<a class="thumb" href="https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/10/fujifilm-to-boost-tape-production-5x-as-ai-fuels-archiving-demand/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/GettyImages-2202649793-2560x1440.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='arstechnica.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">39</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/10/fujifilm-to-boost-tape-production-5x-as-ai-fuels-archiving-demand/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fujifilm to boost tape production 5x as AI fuels archiving demand</span><span class='zh'>AI 推动归档需求，富士胶片将磁带产量提高 5 倍</span></a><span class="dom">arstechnica.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fujifilm plans to invest about 10 billion yen, or roughly $63 million, to increase magnetic-tape production capacity fivefold.</span><span class='zh'>富士胶片计划投资约 100 亿日元，即约 6300 万美元，将磁带产能提高五倍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fujifilm plans to invest about 10 billion yen, or roughly $63 million, to increase magnetic-tape production capacity fivefold. The company attributes the rising demand for archiving to generative AI and what it calls exponential global data growth. The fivefold increase will be measured against fiscal 2025 production levels.</span><span class='zh'>富士胶片计划投资约 100 亿日元，即约 6300 万美元，将磁带产能提高五倍。公司将归档需求的增长归因于生成式 AI 以及其所称的全球数据指数级增长。五倍增幅将以 2025 财年的产量为基准。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50366117" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ask HN: What&#x27;s your current AI agentic setup?</span><span class='zh'>Ask HN：你目前的 AI agent 使用配置是什么？</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An Ask HN thread asks how people use AI agents in their daily work.</span><span class='zh'>一则 Ask HN 帖子询问人们目前如何在日常工作中使用 AI agent。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An Ask HN thread asks how people use AI agents in their daily work. It solicits details about paid subscriptions, external or locally hosted models, coding harnesses, orchestration tools, and broader agent platforms. The discussion aims to gauge how individual workflows have changed over the past year.</span><span class='zh'>一则 Ask HN 帖子询问人们目前如何在日常工作中使用 AI agent。发帖者希望了解大家购买的订阅、外部或本地部署的模型、编程工具链、编排工具以及更广泛的 agent 平台。讨论旨在了解个人工作流在过去一年中的变化。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://martinfowler.com/bliki/LLM-Voice.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LLM Voice</span><span class='zh'>大型语言模型的语音交互</span></a><span class="dom">martinfowler.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LLM Voice examines how language models function in voice-based interfaces, separate from text prompts, emphasizing the need to address speech recognition, latency, and user expectations.</span><span class='zh'>LLM 在语音场景中的交互方式需要和文本输入不同的设计原则。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LLM Voice examines how language models function in voice-based interfaces, separate from text prompts, emphasizing the need to address speech recognition, latency, and user expectations. It argues for treating voice as a distinct modality with tailored UX and safety considerations.</span><span class='zh'>LLM 在语音场景中的交互方式需要和文本输入不同的设计原则。本文强调处理语音识别、延迟和用户预期的重要性，并提出面向语音的 UX 和安全性设计要点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/jiahaoshao1/status/2116475922190922088" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic blocks users for anything touching training larger models</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 屏蔽任何涉及训练更大模型的用户</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A user says Anthropic blocked their account after flagging work involving reinforcement fine-tuning, supervised fine-tuning, or training larger models as “training a competitor.” The user says Claude only helped submit jobs and analyze results, while the rollouts came from an open-source model.</span><span class='zh'>一名用户称，Anthropic 将其涉及强化微调、监督微调或训练更大模型的工作标记为“训练竞争对手”，并屏蔽了其账户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A user says Anthropic blocked their account after flagging work involving reinforcement fine-tuning, supervised fine-tuning, or training larger models as “training a competitor.” The user says Claude only helped submit jobs and analyze results, while the rollouts came from an open-source model. They also report being downgraded from Opus 5.5.</span><span class='zh'>一名用户称，Anthropic 将其涉及强化微调、监督微调或训练更大模型的工作标记为“训练竞争对手”，并屏蔽了其账户。该用户表示，Claude 只帮助提交任务和分析结果，实际生成过程使用的是开源模型。用户还称自己的账户被降级，无法继续使用 Opus 5.5。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://openai.com/index/a-thousand-fewer-paths-to-root/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/400PQ4WTCOvXwwvk1OMOHv/03970ddeafd22fdbc9832669deacc59c/a-thousand-fewer-paths-to-root-social.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='openai.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">46</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/a-thousand-fewer-paths-to-root/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A thousand fewer paths to root</span><span class='zh'>少一千条通往 root 的路径</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI built an autonomous pipeline that discovered, validated, and generated patches for 948 local privilege-escalation vulnerabilities in the Linux kernel.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 构建了一个自主流水线，用于发现、验证并为 Linux 内核中的 948 个本地提权漏洞生成补丁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI built an autonomous pipeline that discovered, validated, and generated patches for 948 local privilege-escalation vulnerabilities in the Linux kernel. In a single run using its cyber model, the system validated the vulnerabilities and produced patches at an API cost of $1.2 million. The work suggests that some security investigations can increasingly be handled as a systematic automated process.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 构建了一个自主流水线，用于发现、验证并为 Linux 内核中的 948 个本地提权漏洞生成补丁。在一次运行中，该系统借助其网络安全模型完成了漏洞验证和补丁生成，API 成本为 120 万美元。这项工作表明，部分安全研究正逐渐可以作为系统化的自动流程来执行。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Northflank says secret rotation for MySQL and Redis is now available to all customers and advises customers to rotate secrets if they have not already.</span><span class='zh'>Northflank 表示，所有客户现在都可以轮换 MySQL 和 Redis 密钥，并建议尚未操作的客户立即轮换。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Northflank says secret rotation for MySQL and Redis is now available to all customers and advises customers to rotate secrets if they have not already. It has also enabled audit logs for all customers, including free users.</span><span class='zh'>Northflank 表示，所有客户现在都可以轮换 MySQL 和 Redis 密钥，并建议尚未操作的客户立即轮换。该平台还为包括免费用户在内的所有客户启用了审计日志。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://about.doordash.com/en-us/news/doordash-to-acquire-instacart" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DoorDash to acquire Instacart</span><span class='zh'>DoorDash 将收购 Instacart</span></a><span class="dom">about.doordash.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DoorDash announced plans to acquire Instacart, creating a larger player in on-demand grocery delivery.</span><span class='zh'>DoorDash 宣布收购 Instacart，将打造更强的即时送货生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DoorDash announced plans to acquire Instacart, creating a larger player in on-demand grocery delivery. The merger could reshape competition, pricing, and last-mile logistics, while triggering antitrust scrutiny and integration challenges for both platforms.</span><span class='zh'>DoorDash 宣布收购 Instacart，将打造更强的即时送货生态。合并可能改变市场竞争格局、定价与末端配送效率，同时面临潜在的反垄断审查和整合挑战。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://nolanlawson.com/2026/10/28/the-ai-cost-of-javascript-frameworks/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://nolanlawson.com/_astro/frameworks.B14_z7MF_2lv3q1.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nolanlawson.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">49</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nolanlawson.com/2026/10/28/the-ai-cost-of-javascript-frameworks/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The AI cost of JavaScript frameworks</span><span class='zh'>JavaScript 框架的 AI 成本</span></a><span class="dom">nolanlawson.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The discussion challenges the web development community&#x27;s tendency to describe frameworks and tools with unsupported claims such as “blazing fast” or “supercharge your development.” It argues that many choices have historically been justified by developer experience and intuition rather than evidence.</span><span class='zh'>文章质疑 Web 开发社区习惯用“快如闪电”或“显著提升开发效率”等缺乏证据的说法描述框架和工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The discussion challenges the web development community&#x27;s tendency to describe frameworks and tools with unsupported claims such as “blazing fast” or “supercharge your development.” It argues that many choices have historically been justified by developer experience and intuition rather than evidence. The focus is on examining the measurable costs of JavaScript frameworks in an era of AI-assisted development.</span><span class='zh'>文章质疑 Web 开发社区习惯用“快如闪电”或“显著提升开发效率”等缺乏证据的说法描述框架和工具。它指出，许多技术选择长期以来更多是依据开发者体验和直觉，而非实证来论证的。文章将关注点放在 AI 辅助开发时代 JavaScript 框架可衡量的成本上。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thedrive.com/news/my-city-canceled-flocks-contract-flock-ignored-it-so-the-police-chief-unplugged-the-cameras" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Police chief unplugs cameras after Flock ignores contract cancellation</span><span class='zh'>Flock 无视合同取消后，警察局长拔掉了摄像头电源</span></a><span class="dom">thedrive.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Plymouth canceled its contract with Flock after cameras wrongly flagged a couple who were later confronted by police.</span><span class='zh'>Plymouth 市因 Flock 摄像头错误标记一对夫妇、导致警方随后对其采取行动而取消了合同。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Plymouth canceled its contract with Flock after cameras wrongly flagged a couple who were later confronted by police. The city suspended user accounts and formally notified Flock, but the cameras remained installed a month later and the company had not said when it would remove them. Police Chief Eric Fadden said the department had stopped using the Flock ALPR system after the city council’s decision.</span><span class='zh'>Plymouth 市因 Flock 摄像头错误标记一对夫妇、导致警方随后对其采取行动而取消了合同。市政府暂停了用户账户并正式通知 Flock，但一个月后摄像头仍未拆除，公司也没有说明何时移除。警察局长 Eric Fadden 表示，市议会作出决定后，部门已停止使用 Flock 的自动车牌识别系统。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hackertv.org" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: HackerTV – HN&#x27;s most-recommended Youtube videos as 24/7 TV channels</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：HackerTV——把 HN 最常被推荐的 YouTube 视频做成全天候频道</span></a><span class="dom">hackertv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HackerTV turns YouTube videos frequently recommended in Hacker News comments into 24/7 topic channels.</span><span class='zh'>HackerTV 将 Hacker News 评论中经常被推荐的 YouTube 视频整理成全天候主题频道。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HackerTV turns YouTube videos frequently recommended in Hacker News comments into 24/7 topic channels. It includes channels such as DEVTV, SOLDER, RETRO, and ORBIT, along with an HN Prime channel containing the top 400 videos. The project was built in a few hours with Claude Code and also includes a music channel and a late-night channel.</span><span class='zh'>HackerTV 将 Hacker News 评论中经常被推荐的 YouTube 视频整理成全天候主题频道。它包含 DEVTV、SOLDER、RETRO 和 ORBIT 等频道，以及收录前 400 个视频的 HN Prime 频道。项目用 Claude Code 在几小时内完成，还提供音乐频道和深夜频道。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/massive-leak-exposes-41000-homeland-security-workers-including-undercover-ice-agents/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Leak Exposes 41,000 Homeland Security Workers–Including Undercover ICE Agents</span><span class='zh'>泄露事件曝光4.1万名国土安全工作人员，包括卧底 ICE 特工</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A leak exposed details of more than 41,000 employees and contractors at the US Department of Homeland Security and seven component agencies.</span><span class='zh'>一次数据泄露曝光了美国国土安全部及其七个下属机构超过4.1万名员工和承包商的信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A leak exposed details of more than 41,000 employees and contractors at the US Department of Homeland Security and seven component agencies. The records include email addresses, phone numbers, office locations, references to internal systems, and sensitive information about undercover and surveillance programs. The data was last updated on August 19, 2026, and was previewed by Distributed Denial of Secrets and researcher-journalist maia arson crimew. The records reportedly came from a group calling itself the Department of Peace.</span><span class='zh'>一次数据泄露曝光了美国国土安全部及其七个下属机构超过4.1万名员工和承包商的信息。记录包括电子邮件地址、电话号码、办公地点、内部系统信息，以及有关卧底和监控项目的敏感内容。数据最后更新于2026年8月19日，由 Distributed Denial of Secrets 和研究员兼记者 maia arson crimew 预览。两者称数据来自一个自称“和平部”的组织。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece reflects on Patrick Stewart’s portrayal of Captain Jean-Luc Picard and the leadership ideals associated with the character.</span><span class='zh'>文章回顾了帕特里克·斯图尔特饰演让-卢克·皮卡尔舰长时所体现的领导理想。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sidbala.com/h-264-is-magic/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>H.264 Is Magic (2016)</span><span class='zh'>H.264 的魔力（2016）</span></a><span class="dom">sidbala.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>H.264 Is Magic (2016) analyzes why the H.264 video codec became a de-facto standard, enabling efficient compression, broad hardware support, and widespread adoption.</span><span class='zh'>H.264 的魔力（2016）解析为何 H.264 成为事实标准、实现高效压缩与广泛的硬件支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>H.264 Is Magic (2016) analyzes why the H.264 video codec became a de-facto standard, enabling efficient compression, broad hardware support, and widespread adoption. It discusses licensing realities that helped lock in the standard and powered decades of streaming and video apps.</span><span class='zh'>H.264 的魔力（2016）解析为何 H.264 成为事实标准、实现高效压缩与广泛的硬件支持。文章还讨论了授权环境如何将该标准锁定并推动多年来的流媒体与视频应用发展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hackertimes.ca/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Hacker Times, a New York Times-inspired version of Hacker News</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Hacker Times，一份受《纽约时报》启发的 Hacker News 版本</span></a><span class="dom">hackertimes.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hacker Times presents Hacker News in a newspaper-style interface.</span><span class='zh'>Hacker Times 用报纸式界面呈现 Hacker News。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hacker Times presents Hacker News in a newspaper-style interface. The site updates once per hour.</span><span class='zh'>Hacker Times 用报纸式界面呈现 Hacker News。该网站每小时更新一次。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/guregodevo/memdoor/tree/main/examples/workflows/team-solve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Memdoor: A team of 5 Qwen3.8-27B models beats Fable 5 on LiveCodeBench Hard</span><span class='zh'>Memdoor：五个 Qwen3.8-27B 模型在 LiveCodeBench Hard 上击败 Fable 5</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Memdoor implements a team-solving workflow in which five small models act as a planner, ideators, solver, manager, and repair worker.</span><span class='zh'>Memdoor 实现了一种协作解题流程，由五个小模型分别担任规划者、创意提出者、求解器、管理者和修复者。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Memdoor implements a team-solving workflow in which five small models act as a planner, ideators, solver, manager, and repair worker. Each model starts with a fresh context and communicates only through files, while an external script determines whether solutions pass. The authors reproduced the method using Qwen3.8-27B, eight YAML files, their prompts, and a 128K response limit.</span><span class='zh'>Memdoor 实现了一种协作解题流程，由五个小模型分别担任规划者、创意提出者、求解器、管理者和修复者。每个模型都从全新的上下文开始，仅通过磁盘文件通信，并由外部脚本判断解答是否通过。作者使用 Qwen3.8-27B、八个 YAML 文件、自定义提示词和 128K 回复上限复现了这一方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theverge.com/news/1027379/discord-ditching-electron-web-app" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Discord is ditching Electron and its new app uses less RAM</span><span class='zh'>Discord 放弃 Electron，新应用占用更少内存</span></a><span class="dom">theverge.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Discord is moving its applications fully to Rust and abandoning Electron for the desktop client.</span><span class='zh'>Discord 正将其应用全面迁移到 Rust，并放弃桌面客户端使用的 Electron。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Discord is moving its applications fully to Rust and abandoning Electron for the desktop client. The company says the change will remove Electron, while mobile keeps a native UI and shares application code with desktop through Rust. Discord has used Rust in infrastructure, voice technology, and parts of its client since 2018.</span><span class='zh'>Discord 正将其应用全面迁移到 Rust，并放弃桌面客户端使用的 Electron。公司表示，此举将移除 Electron；移动端继续使用原生 UI，并通过 Rust 与桌面端共享应用代码。Discord 自 2018 年起已在基础设施、语音技术和部分客户端中使用 Rust。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>427</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50378625' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>367</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50296486" data-id="50296486" data-tags="AI|Gaming" data-score="421" data-comments="171" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296486" data-time="1793310028">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://labs.ycombinator.com/projects/games/index.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We ran AI agent swarms in Minecraft and Mindustry</span><span class='zh'>在 Minecraft 与 Mindustry 中测试 AI 代理群</span></a><span class="dom">labs.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers ran AI agent swarms in Minecraft and Mindustry to study emergent coordination at scale.</span><span class='zh'>在 Minecraft 与 Mindustry 中测试AI代理群，研究群体协作与尺度效应。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers ran AI agent swarms in Minecraft and Mindustry to study emergent coordination at scale. The experiments illustrate how simple agents, when put in shared environments, can exhibit complex group behaviors, informing the design of scalable multi-agent systems and game AI safety. The work provides hands-on demos for researchers building autonomous agents in synthetic worlds.</span><span class='zh'>在 Minecraft 与 Mindustry 中测试AI代理群，研究群体协作与尺度效应。实验表明，简单代理在共享环境中可涌现出复杂的协同行为，为多智能体系统和游戏AI安全提供直观范例。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Gaming' style='--c:#a855f7'>Gaming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/29/top_stories_10292026'><span class='en'>Oct 29</span><span class='zh'>10月29日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>421</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296486' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>171</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50352758" data-id="50352758" data-tags="Hardware|DevOps" data-score="413" data-comments="224" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50352758" data-time="1793717622">
<a class="thumb" href="https://namespace.so/blog/mac-at-any-scale" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://namespace.so/blog-images/mac-at-any-scale/banner.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='namespace.so' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://namespace.so/blog/mac-at-any-scale" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mac at any scale</span><span class='zh'>Mac 的任意规模化部署</span></a><span class="dom">namespace.so</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project describes what is required to run Macs in a data center, including custom MacBook racks, macOS virtualization with Apple’s Virtualization framework, and distributing large virtual-machine images across a fleet.</span><span class='zh'>该项目介绍了在数据中心运行 Mac 所需的技术，包括定制 MacBook 机架、使用 Apple Virtualization 框架虚拟化 macOS，以及在设备集群中分发大型虚拟机镜像。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project describes what is required to run Macs in a data center, including custom MacBook racks, macOS virtualization with Apple’s Virtualization framework, and distributing large virtual-machine images across a fleet. The author says hundreds of MacBook Pros were unpacked for new server racks. The work explores how Mac infrastructure can be deployed at scale.</span><span class='zh'>该项目介绍了在数据中心运行 Mac 所需的技术，包括定制 MacBook 机架、使用 Apple Virtualization 框架虚拟化 macOS，以及在设备集群中分发大型虚拟机镜像。作者表示，已拆箱数百台 MacBook Pro，用于搭建新的服务器机架。项目探索了如何大规模部署 Mac 基础设施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://markblah.github.io/lemmings/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lemmings CSS Animation</span><span class='zh'>旅鼠 CSS 动画</span></a><span class="dom">markblah.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This interactive CSS animation lets users add walkers with a button or with `__walker.add()`.</span><span class='zh'>这个交互式 CSS 动画允许用户通过按钮或 `__walker.add()` 添加行走者。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This interactive CSS animation lets users add walkers with a button or with `__walker.add()`. Walkers can be dragged, carried, or flicked through the scene. Resizing the window makes them ride the surface beneath them, while narrowing the window wraps them onto new rows and pushes walkers standing on those rows.</span><span class='zh'>这个交互式 CSS 动画允许用户通过按钮或 `__walker.add()` 添加行走者。行走者可以被拖动、携带或弹射。调整窗口大小时，它们会沿脚下的表面移动；窗口变窄后，行走者会换到新行，并推动站在其上的其他行走者。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.techpolicy.press/the-sec-should-block-anthropics-ipo-to-protect-the-public/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The SEC Should Block Anthropic&#x27;s IPO to Protect the Public</span><span class='zh'>SEC 应阻止 Anthropic IPO 以保护公众</span></a><span class="dom">techpolicy.press</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Matt Scherer argues that the SEC should block a potential Anthropic public offering.</span><span class='zh'>Matt Scherer 主张 SEC 应阻止 Anthropic 可能进行的 IPO。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Matt Scherer argues that the SEC should block a potential Anthropic public offering. He says workers, retail investors, and retirees could bear the losses if the company&#x27;s bills come due, and identifies the argument with a report about protecting people and the economy from an AI-bubble collapse.</span><span class='zh'>Matt Scherer 主张 SEC 应阻止 Anthropic 可能进行的 IPO。他认为，如果公司的账单最终无法承担，员工、散户投资者和退休人员可能会承受损失；这一观点与一份讨论如何在 AI 泡沫破裂时保护公众和经济的报告相关。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://go.dev/blog/jsonv2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A new Go API for JSON</span><span class='zh'>Go 推出新的 JSON API</span></a><span class="dom">go.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Go 1.27 introduces the new encoding/json/v2 and encoding/json/jsontext packages.</span><span class='zh'>Go 1.27 引入了新的 encoding/json/v2 和 encoding/json/jsontext 包。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Go 1.27 introduces the new encoding/json/v2 and encoding/json/jsontext packages. JSON is the most widely used data format on the internet and has been supported by Go’s standard library since before Go 1.0. The new packages extend Go’s standard-library support for reading and writing JSON.</span><span class='zh'>Go 1.27 引入了新的 encoding/json/v2 和 encoding/json/jsontext 包。JSON 是互联网上使用最广泛的数据格式，Go 从 1.0 之前就已在标准库中提供支持。这些新包进一步扩展了 Go 标准库读写 JSON 的能力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Patrick Stewart’s portrayal of Captain Jean-Luc Picard presented a complex character who strived to do what was right.</span><span class='zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特饰演的让·吕克·皮卡尔舰长，是一个努力做正确之事的复杂人物。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Patrick Stewart’s portrayal of Captain Jean-Luc Picard presented a complex character who strived to do what was right. The character influenced a generation of young viewers.</span><span class='zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特饰演的让·吕克·皮卡尔舰长，是一个努力做正确之事的复杂人物。这个角色影响了一代年轻观众。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rakhim.exotext.com/i-wish-tech-companies-knew-more-about-me" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I wish tech companies knew more about me</span><span class='zh'>我希望科技公司更了解我</span></a><span class="dom">rakhim.exotext.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author reflects on the common claim that technology companies spy on people and know intimate details about their lives.</span><span class='zh'>作者反思了“科技公司在监视人们、了解其生活隐私”这一常见说法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author reflects on the common claim that technology companies spy on people and know intimate details about their lives. Despite that widespread narrative, the author says they have never personally felt anything close to being known in that way. The piece contrasts public assumptions about surveillance with that personal experience.</span><span class='zh'>作者反思了“科技公司在监视人们、了解其生活隐私”这一常见说法。尽管这种叙事十分普遍，作者表示自己从未有过被如此了解的切身感受。文章将公众对监控的普遍想象与作者的个人经历进行了对比。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kevinraymond/florilegium" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Florilegium: Procedural Flowers in LaTeX</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium：用 LaTeX 生成程序化花卉装饰</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Florilegium generates Delft-blue floral page margins for LuaLaTeX using Lua.</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium 使用 Lua 为 LuaLaTeX 生成代尔夫特蓝风格的花卉页面边饰。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Florilegium generates Delft-blue floral page margins for LuaLaTeX using Lua. It combines hand-authored primitives, randomized parameters, and a procedural layout that fills unused space. The creator says the project produces a similar look to a referenced design without trying to replicate it, and used Claude during development.</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium 使用 Lua 为 LuaLaTeX 生成代尔夫特蓝风格的花卉页面边饰。它结合手工编写的基本图形、随机参数和用于填充空白区域的程序化布局。作者表示，该项目形成了与某个参考设计相似的效果，但并未试图复制原作，开发过程中使用了 Claude。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>378</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305637' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>54</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50372945" data-id="50372945" data-tags="AI" data-score="377" data-comments="233" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50372945" data-time="1793835344">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/introducing-images-2-6/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Images 2.6</span><span class='zh'>Images 2.6</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Images 2.6 introduces faster image generation, GIF creation, more precise editing, and new API controls in ChatGPT, Codex, and the API.</span><span class='zh'>Images 2.6 为 ChatGPT、Codex 和 API 带来了更快的图像生成、GIF 创建、更精确的编辑以及新的 API 控制功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Images 2.6 introduces faster image generation, GIF creation, more precise editing, and new API controls in ChatGPT, Codex, and the API. Users can animate images such as brand mascots, characters, and product shots into looping GIFs. The update also aims to preserve characters, products, and brand details more consistently across editing rounds.</span><span class='zh'>Images 2.6 为 ChatGPT、Codex 和 API 带来了更快的图像生成、GIF 创建、更精确的编辑以及新的 API 控制功能。用户可以将品牌吉祥物、角色和产品图像制作成循环 GIF。此次更新还旨在让角色、产品和品牌细节在多轮编辑中保持更一致。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sw.kovidgoyal.net/kitty/changelog/#" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kitty 0.50</span><span class='zh'>Kitty 0.50</span></a><span class="dom">sw.kovidgoyal.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kitty 0.50 adds docked windows that can attach to the edge of another window or tab.</span><span class='zh'>Kitty 0.50 增加了停靠窗口，可附着在另一个窗口或标签页的边缘。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kitty 0.50 adds docked windows that can attach to the edge of another window or tab. Docks sit outside the main layout and can serve as status bars, file trees, or shells beside an editor. They follow their host window across tabs, close with it, and are saved in sessions.</span><span class='zh'>Kitty 0.50 增加了停靠窗口，可附着在另一个窗口或标签页的边缘。停靠窗口位于主布局之外，适合用作状态栏、文件树或编辑器旁的 Shell。它会随宿主窗口切换标签页，在宿主关闭时一同关闭，并保存到会话中。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.interdb.jp/pg/index.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Internals of PostgreSQL</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL 内部机制</span></a><span class="dom">interdb.jp</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The item concerns the internals of PostgreSQL.</span><span class='zh'>该项目关注 PostgreSQL 的内部机制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The item concerns the internals of PostgreSQL. The available material provides no technical discussion of PostgreSQL itself.</span><span class='zh'>该项目关注 PostgreSQL 的内部机制。现有内容没有提供 PostgreSQL 本身的技术介绍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.parameter.ai/labs/we-found-a-gap-at-openai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Do the math: How we took over ChatGPT accounts</span><span class='zh'>我们接管了 ChatGPT 账户</span></a><span class="dom">parameter.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers bypassed ChatGPT’s sandbox and content security policy to take over accounts by sending victims a link.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员绕过了 ChatGPT 的沙箱和内容安全策略，只需向受害者发送链接即可接管账户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers bypassed ChatGPT’s sandbox and content security policy to take over accounts by sending victims a link. In October 2026, they found that a public ChatGPT share could be turned into attacker-controlled JavaScript running inside the signed-in session of anyone who opened it. An attacker could publish a malicious conversation through ChatGPT’s normal sharing feature, and the payload would execute on chatgpt.com with access to the victim’s authenticated web session.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员绕过了 ChatGPT 的沙箱和内容安全策略，只需向受害者发送链接即可接管账户。2026 年 10 月，他们发现可以将普通的 ChatGPT 公开分享转换为攻击者控制的 JavaScript，并在打开链接者的登录会话中运行。攻击者可以通过 ChatGPT 的正常分享功能发布恶意对话，载荷随后会以 chatgpt.com 的身份执行，并获得受害者已认证的网页会话权限。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>361</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50314699' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>176</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50313661" data-id="50313661" data-tags="Culture" data-score="358" data-comments="267" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50313661" data-time="1793427396">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://vitalik.eth.limo/general/2026/10/31/digital_minimalism.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Digital minimalism</span><span class='zh'>数字极简主义</span></a><span class="dom">vitalik.eth.limo</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author says they have long favored physical minimalism, owning few possessions and doing more with less.</span><span class='zh'>作者表示，自己长期推崇物质极简主义，即减少实体物品并以更少资源完成更多事情。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author says they have long favored physical minimalism, owning few possessions and doing more with less. They argue that the idea responded to the conditions of the 2000s and 2010s, while still retaining value today. The author proposes digital minimalism as a new form suited to the modern era.</span><span class='zh'>作者表示，自己长期推崇物质极简主义，即减少实体物品并以更少资源完成更多事情。作者认为，这一理念回应了 21 世纪初和 2010 年代的环境，但如今仍有价值。作者进一步提出，现代社会需要一种新的极简主义：数字极简主义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50312756" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: Scribd Breached</span><span class='zh'>Tell HN：Scribd 遭到入侵</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Scribd notified users of a security incident in which an unauthorized party may have accessed data in one of its systems.</span><span class='zh'>Scribd 通知用户称，其一个系统发生安全事件，未经授权的第三方可能访问了部分数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Scribd notified users of a security incident in which an unauthorized party may have accessed data in one of its systems. Potentially exposed information includes email addresses, the last four digits and country associated with a credit card, card issuer, expiry date, and payment history, but not full card details. The company said it had no evidence of misuse and warned users to watch for phishing.</span><span class='zh'>Scribd 通知用户称，其一个系统发生安全事件，未经授权的第三方可能访问了部分数据。可能暴露的信息包括电子邮件地址、信用卡末四位及所属国家、发卡机构、有效期和支付历史，但不包括完整银行卡信息。公司表示没有证据表明这些数据已被滥用，并提醒用户警惕钓鱼攻击。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.businessinsider.com/anthropic-claude-internal-ai-use-exceeds-employee-pay-tokens-2026-11" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s internal AI use, at retail prices, now exceeds its employee pay</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的内部 AI 使用量按零售价计算已超过员工薪酬</span></a><span class="dom">businessinsider.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s IPO filing reportedly shows that the retail value of its internal AI usage has exceeded the company&#x27;s employee compensation.</span><span class='zh'>据 Anthropic 的 IPO 文件披露，按零售价计算，该公司内部 AI 的使用量已经超过员工薪酬总额。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s IPO filing reportedly shows that the retail value of its internal AI usage has exceeded the company&#x27;s employee compensation. The figure is presented as a milestone in the company&#x27;s internal use of AI.</span><span class='zh'>据 Anthropic 的 IPO 文件披露，按零售价计算，该公司内部 AI 的使用量已经超过员工薪酬总额。这一数字被视为 Anthropic 内部使用 AI 的一个里程碑。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/JiaFeiMiao-K-Cat/DiscImageStudio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DiscImageStudio: burn visible artwork onto CDs, DVDs and Blu-ray discs</span><span class='zh'>DiscImageStudio：将可见图案刻录到 CD、DVD 和蓝光光盘</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Disc Image Studio converts an image into data that conforms to the physical-layer constraints of CDs, DVDs, and single-layer BD-R discs.</span><span class='zh'>Disc Image Studio 可将图片转换为符合 CD、DVD 和单层 BD-R 光盘物理层约束的数据，刻录后光盘表面会呈现可见图案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Disc Image Studio converts an image into data that conforms to the physical-layer constraints of CDs, DVDs, and single-layer BD-R discs. After burning, the disc surface displays a visible pattern. The Windows desktop version supports design and burning, while Linux and macOS have experimental offline command-line versions.</span><span class='zh'>Disc Image Studio 可将图片转换为符合 CD、DVD 和单层 BD-R 光盘物理层约束的数据，刻录后光盘表面会呈现可见图案。Windows 桌面版支持设计与刻录，Linux 和 macOS 则提供实验性的离线命令行版本。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://fortune.com/article/why-did-us-workers-just-take-home-smallest-share-of-capital-since-1947-lowest-ever-recorded/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/GettyImages-1322099863-e1768339483475.jpg?resize=1200,600' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='fortune.com' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">80</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fortune.com/article/why-did-us-workers-just-take-home-smallest-share-of-capital-since-1947-lowest-ever-recorded/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>U.S. workers took home their smallest share of capital since at least 1947</span><span class='zh'>美国工人获得的资本份额至少降至 1947 年以来最低</span></a><span class="dom">fortune.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>U.S. labor share fell to 52.8% of economic output in the second quarter of 2026, the lowest level since the Bureau of Labor Statistics began recording the measure in 1947.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年第二季度，美国劳动者获得的经济产出份额降至 52.8%，为美国劳工统计局 1947 年开始记录该指标以来的最低水平。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>U.S. labor share fell to 52.8% of economic output in the second quarter of 2026, the lowest level since the Bureau of Labor Statistics began recording the measure in 1947. It was 53.6% in the previous quarter, compared with an average of about 55.6% for the decade. The decline occurred as corporate earnings and GDP grew while workers received a smaller share through wages and salaries.</span><span class='zh'>2026 年第二季度，美国劳动者获得的经济产出份额降至 52.8%，为美国劳工统计局 1947 年开始记录该指标以来的最低水平。上一季度这一比例为 53.6%，本十年的平均值约为 55.6%。与此同时，企业利润和 GDP 增长，但劳动者通过工资和薪酬获得的份额下降。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>341</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50330939' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>208</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50322173" data-id="50322173" data-tags="Legal|Security|AI" data-score="339" data-comments="214" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50322173" data-time="1793499593">
<a class="thumb" href="https://jetstream.security/insights/brazilian-court-sanctions-prompt-injection-filing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://jetstream.security/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/ChatGPT-Image-Jul-1-2026-02_56_34-PM.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='jetstream.security' data-c='#64748b' data-tag='Legal'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">81</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jetstream.security/insights/brazilian-court-sanctions-prompt-injection-filing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Brazilian Court Sanctions Lawyers for Prompt Injection in Court Filing</span><span class='zh'>巴西法院因诉状中的提示注入惩罚律师</span></a><span class="dom">jetstream.security</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Two lawyers embedded a white-on-white instruction in a court filing telling an AI to treat their case leniently.</span><span class='zh'>两名律师在法院文件中嵌入了白底白字的指令，要求 AI 对他们的案件从轻处理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Two lawyers embedded a white-on-white instruction in a court filing telling an AI to treat their case leniently. The court&#x27;s AI document-review system detected the hidden prompt injection. The court fined the lawyers $16,000.</span><span class='zh'>两名律师在法院文件中嵌入了白底白字的指令，要求 AI 对他们的案件从轻处理。法院的 AI 文档审查系统检测到了这条隐藏的提示注入指令。法院因此对两名律师处以 16,000 美元罚款。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://wikihack.netlify.app" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Wikihack – HackerTube, but for Wikipedia articles mentioned on HN</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Wikihack——面向 HN 提及维基百科文章的 HackerTube</span></a><span class="dom">wikihack.netlify.app</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wikihack scrapes Wikipedia links mentioned on Hacker News and turns them into a ranked, scrollable feed.</span><span class='zh'>Wikihack 抓取 Hacker News 上提到的维基百科链接，并将其整理成可滚动的排名信息流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wikihack scrapes Wikipedia links mentioned on Hacker News and turns them into a ranked, scrollable feed. It shows where each article was submitted or mentioned in comments, with views for top, randomly weighted, and recently rising links. The site is currently served entirely from the frontend, with more features planned.</span><span class='zh'>Wikihack 抓取 Hacker News 上提到的维基百科链接，并将其整理成可滚动的排名信息流。它会显示每篇文章在哪些投稿或评论中出现，并提供热门、加权随机和近 12 个月上升趋势等视图。目前网站完全由前端提供服务，未来还计划加入更多功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://criminallyvulgar.dev/you-should-absolutely-download-your-favourite-youtube-videos/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Download your favourite YouTube videos</span><span class='zh'>下载你最喜欢的 YouTube 视频</span></a><span class="dom">criminallyvulgar.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author recommends downloading and archiving online videos that matter to you because content can disappear from services such as YouTube.</span><span class='zh'>作者建议下载并归档对自己重要的在线视频，因为 YouTube 等服务上的内容可能会消失。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author recommends downloading and archiving online videos that matter to you because content can disappear from services such as YouTube. They note that old liked videos frequently become unavailable. Tools such as yt-dlp, combined with a local drive or NAS, are presented as a simple way to preserve access.</span><span class='zh'>作者建议下载并归档对自己重要的在线视频，因为 YouTube 等服务上的内容可能会消失。作者指出，早期点赞的视频经常会变得无法访问。yt-dlp 等工具配合本地硬盘或 NAS，就能以较简单的方式保留访问权。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nist.gov/news-events/news/2026/11/self-employed-solopreneur-nist-has-cybersecurity-guide-just-you" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Self-Employed? Solopreneur? NIST Has a Cybersecurity Guide Just for You</span><span class='zh'>个体户？单人创业者？NIST 为你准备了网络安全指南</span></a><span class="dom">nist.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The guide is designed to help solopreneurs prioritize and implement selected actions from the NIST Cybersecurity Framework.</span><span class='zh'>这份指南旨在帮助单人创业者从 NIST 网络安全框架中确定优先级，并落实其中适合自身业务的一组措施。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The guide is designed to help solopreneurs prioritize and implement selected actions from the NIST Cybersecurity Framework. It focuses on measures most useful for protecting small businesses.</span><span class='zh'>这份指南旨在帮助单人创业者从 NIST 网络安全框架中确定优先级，并落实其中适合自身业务的一组措施。其重点是帮助小型企业采取更有针对性的安全行动。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/03/top_stories_11032026'><span class='en'>Nov 3</span><span class='zh'>11月3日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>324</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50340619' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>91</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50263837" data-id="50263837" data-tags="Web|Politics|AI|Business" data-score="323" data-comments="144" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50263837" data-time="1793126575">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.techpolicy.press/breaking-the-open-web-wont-stop-the-bad-bots/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/3tzzh18d/production/46070e9d00c44c7690c79155723ff59e5c1280fe-1200x675.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='techpolicy.press' data-c='#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">89</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.techpolicy.press/breaking-the-open-web-wont-stop-the-bad-bots/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Breaking the open web won&#x27;t stop the bad bots</span><span class='zh'>破坏开放网络并不能阻止恶意机器人</span></a><span class="dom">techpolicy.press</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The authors argue that policymakers should not treat anonymity itself as a problem.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，政策制定者不应把匿名本身视为问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The authors argue that policymakers should not treat anonymity itself as a problem. They acknowledge that AI developers’ automated crawling is depriving newsrooms of referral traffic and advertising revenue, but say the proposed Stealth Bot Prohibition Act would require automatic identification from anyone accessing a website. In their view, that approach fails to target abusive crawling and threatens a basic tool used to gather public information.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，政策制定者不应把匿名本身视为问题。他们承认，AI 开发者的自动抓取正在让新闻编辑室失去引荐流量和广告收入，但指出拟议的《隐形机器人禁令法》会要求所有自动访问网站的人表明身份及用途。在作者看来，这种做法没有直接针对滥用式抓取，反而会威胁人们获取公开信息的基本工具。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>323</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50263837' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>144</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50305573" data-id="50305573" data-tags="Programming|Open Source" data-score="323" data-comments="94" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305573" data-time="1793375069">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tangled.org/blog/forge/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The federated code forge</span><span class='zh'>联邦式代码托管平台</span></a><span class="dom">tangled.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that centralized code forges, which host code and coordinate reviews, are becoming less dependable.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为，负责托管代码和协调评审的中心化代码平台正变得不再可靠。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that centralized code forges, which host code and coordinate reviews, are becoming less dependable. It presents Git combined with email as an open alternative that has endured over time, although this approach sacrifices some user experience. The proposed direction is a more federated model for code collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为，负责托管代码和协调评审的中心化代码平台正变得不再可靠。它将 Git 与电子邮件结合的方式视为经受时间考验的开放替代方案，尽管这种方式会牺牲部分用户体验。文章提出的方向是更加联邦化的代码协作模式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://smsofdeath.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zero click, silent, remote Android DoS</span><span class='zh'>零点击静默的远程 Android 拒绝服务攻击</span></a><span class="dom">smsofdeath.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A zero-click, silent remote DoS vulnerability on Android enables denial-of-service without user interaction, potentially affecting many devices.</span><span class='zh'>一个无需用户交互、静默发生的 Android 远程拒绝服务漏洞，可能影响大量设备。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A zero-click, silent remote DoS vulnerability on Android enables denial-of-service without user interaction, potentially affecting many devices. Exploits could be triggered via crafted traffic or messages, highlighting the urgent need for timely OS patches and mitigations.</span><span class='zh'>一个无需用户交互、静默发生的 Android 远程拒绝服务漏洞，可能影响大量设备。利用方式可能通过特制网络流量或消息触发，凸显了及时打补丁与缓解措施的紧迫性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nypost.com/2026/10/29/business/alleged-chinese-spy-had-close-relationship-with-nvidia-boss-jensen-huang-report/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Alleged Chinese spy had &#x27;close relationship&#x27; with Nvidia boss Jensen Huang</span><span class='zh'>疑似中国间谍与黄仁勋关系密切</span></a><span class="dom">nypost.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An alleged Chinese intelligence asset is reported to have forged a close relationship with Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang, fueling concerns about insider risk and IP protection in the AI hardware race.</span><span class='zh'>一份报道声称一名中国情报人员与 Nvidia 创始人黄仁勋关系密切，引发对内部人风险和知识产权保护的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An alleged Chinese intelligence asset is reported to have forged a close relationship with Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang, fueling concerns about insider risk and IP protection in the AI hardware race. The episode underscores how executive networks can become vectors for state-linked influence, pushing firms to tighten access controls and disclosures around partnerships. For technologists, it&#x27;s a reminder to guard sensitive collaborations and monitor for unusual access patterns.</span><span class='zh'>一份报道声称一名中国情报人员与 Nvidia 创始人黄仁勋关系密切，引发对内部人风险和知识产权保护的担忧。此事揭示高层网络可能成为国家利益争夺的渠道，促使企业加强访问控制与合作披露。对技术公司而言，需谨慎管理敏感合作与异常访问。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/saragordic/window-sweaters" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Window Sweaters</span><span class='zh'>Window Sweaters</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Window Sweaters is a macOS menu-bar app that adds knitted borders to application windows.</span><span class='zh'>Window Sweaters 是一款 macOS 菜单栏应用，会为应用窗口添加针织风格的边框。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Window Sweaters is a macOS menu-bar app that adds knitted borders to application windows. It offers sweater-like borders and colors inspired by different apps. The project can also be handed to a coding agent to install.</span><span class='zh'>Window Sweaters 是一款 macOS 菜单栏应用，会为应用窗口添加针织风格的边框。它提供类似毛衣的边框和受不同应用启发的配色。项目还可以交给编程代理来完成安装。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://p.migdal.pl/warsaw-vibe-voxel/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Warsaw in voxels – An interactive 3D map of the city centre</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：体素化的华沙——市中心互动 3D 地图</span></a><span class="dom">p.migdal.pl</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project renders central Warsaw as an interactive voxel map inspired by Minecraft and MagicaVoxel.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目以受 Minecraft 和 MagicaVoxel 启发的体素形式呈现华沙市中心互动地图。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project renders central Warsaw as an interactive voxel map inspired by Minecraft and MagicaVoxel. Users can change the date, time, and weather, explore the city, or control individual pedestrians. It combines OpenStreetMap, Polish geospatial and LiDAR data, aerial imagery, and Warsaw&#x27;s tree database, and is open source.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目以受 Minecraft 和 MagicaVoxel 启发的体素形式呈现华沙市中心互动地图。用户可以改变日期、时间和天气，探索城市，或控制单个行人。项目结合了 OpenStreetMap、波兰地理与 LiDAR 数据、航拍图像以及华沙城市树木数据库，并已开源。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fortune.com/2026/10/30/cisco-president-jeetu-patel-gen-z-27-year-old-unlimited-ai-tokens-rewrite-legacy-code-entry-level-jobs/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cisco gives engineer unlimited AI tokens to rewrite legacy code</span><span class='zh'>思科给工程师无限 AI 令牌，用于重写遗留代码</span></a><span class="dom">fortune.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cisco president and chief product officer Jeetu Patel gave a 27-year-old employee named Paddy unlimited AI tokens to refactor code.</span><span class='zh'>思科总裁兼首席产品官 Jeetu Patel 给一名叫 Paddy 的 27 岁员工无限 AI 令牌，让他重构代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cisco president and chief product officer Jeetu Patel gave a 27-year-old employee named Paddy unlimited AI tokens to refactor code. Patel described Paddy as an “energizer bunny” and told him to use the tokens freely. The assignment is presented against a backdrop in which some employers plan to hire fewer graduates because AI can perform certain work more cheaply.</span><span class='zh'>思科总裁兼首席产品官 Jeetu Patel 给一名叫 Paddy 的 27 岁员工无限 AI 令牌，让他重构代码。Patel 称 Paddy 是一台“永动机”，并让他放手使用这些令牌。这项安排发生在一些雇主计划减少应届毕业生招聘的背景下，因为他们认为 AI 能以更低成本完成部分工作。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>311</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50304122' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>382</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50323123" data-id="50323123" data-tags="AI" data-score="311" data-comments="158" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50323123" data-time="1793513610">
<a class="thumb" href="https://knlb.dev/essays/surrender" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://knlb.dev/essays/.surrender_assets/image-4147323edcec4900b374ba7a6e8d4cbb.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='knlb.dev' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">97</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://knlb.dev/essays/surrender" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Resisting Cognitive Surrender</span><span class='zh'>抵抗认知投降</span></a><span class="dom">knlb.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The note examines “cognitive surrender”: accepting agent outputs uncritically without thinking, exploring alternatives, or checking assumptions.</span><span class='zh'>这篇笔记讨论“认知投降”：不加批判地接受智能体输出，不再思考、探索或考虑替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The note examines “cognitive surrender”: accepting agent outputs uncritically without thinking, exploring alternatives, or checking assumptions. It describes this behavior as fast and effortless, which makes it especially seductive.</span><span class='zh'>这篇笔记讨论“认知投降”：不加批判地接受智能体输出，不再思考、探索或考虑替代方案。作者指出，这种工作方式快速且省力，因此尤其具有诱惑力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chris124567/cfsolve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cloudflare Turnstile solver library (no headless browser required)</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare Turnstile 求解库（无需无头浏览器）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>cfsolve is a Rust library and command-line tool that solves Cloudflare Turnstile widgets and challenge pages without a headless browser.</span><span class='zh'>cfsolve 是一个 Rust 库和命令行工具，无需无头浏览器即可处理 Cloudflare Turnstile 小部件和挑战页面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>cfsolve is a Rust library and command-line tool that solves Cloudflare Turnstile widgets and challenge pages without a headless browser. It returns a Turnstile token for widgets or a cf_clearance cookie for challenge pages. Widgets can be handled with a page URL and site key, while challenge pages require a direct fetch or the page’s HTML.</span><span class='zh'>cfsolve 是一个 Rust 库和命令行工具，无需无头浏览器即可处理 Cloudflare Turnstile 小部件和挑战页面。它会为小部件返回 Turnstile 令牌，为挑战页面返回 cf_clearance Cookie。处理小部件需要页面 URL 和站点密钥，而挑战页面则需要直接抓取页面或提供页面 HTML。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>310</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50277379' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>108</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50325253" data-id="50325253" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="310" data-comments="94" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50325253" data-time="1793538150">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ivankovic/omnidiff" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OmniDiff – Opensource, super fast, content aware Git difftool, in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：OmniDiff——用 Rust 编写的开源高速内容感知 Git 差异工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OmniDiff is an AGPLv3 open-source diff tool written in Rust that handles code, configuration, images, PDFs, archives, cursors, and fonts.</span><span class='zh'>OmniDiff 是一个采用 AGPLv3 许可、用 Rust 编写的开源差异工具，支持代码、配置文件、图像、PDF、压缩包、光标和字体等内容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OmniDiff is an AGPLv3 open-source diff tool written in Rust that handles code, configuration, images, PDFs, archives, cursors, and fonts. It can compare C++ files tens of thousands of lines long in under 100 milliseconds on a 2023 ThinkPad T14s. Its pipelines were informed by manually studying 2,000 diffs sampled from 7,400 Gentoo package repositories, and the project is currently at version 0.3.0.</span><span class='zh'>OmniDiff 是一个采用 AGPLv3 许可、用 Rust 编写的开源差异工具，支持代码、配置文件、图像、PDF、压缩包、光标和字体等内容。它在 2023 年款 ThinkPad T14s 上可在不到 100 毫秒内比较数万行的 C++ 文件。其处理流程源于对 7400 个 Gentoo 软件包仓库中抽样的 2000 个差异进行人工研究，目前版本为 0.3.0。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>310</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50325253' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>94</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50287775" data-id="50287775" data-tags="Web" data-score="300" data-comments="416" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50287775" data-time="1793276340">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://iptrust.co/blog/if-ipv4-were-land" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If IPv4 were land</span><span class='zh'>若把 IPv4 当成陆地</span></a><span class="dom">iptrust.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>IPv4 scarcity is depicted as a shrinking landmass with a fixed coastline of addresses.</span><span class='zh'>将 IPv4 的稀缺性比作逐渐缩小的陆地，地址空间如同海岸线般有限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>IPv4 scarcity is depicted as a shrinking landmass with a fixed coastline of addresses. The piece explains why NATs and address transfers matter, and why IPv6 adoption remains essential for scalable growth. It highlights practical implications for network architects and service providers.</span><span class='zh'>将 IPv4 的稀缺性比作逐渐缩小的陆地，地址空间如同海岸线般有限。文章阐述网络地址转换和地址交易的重要性，以及为什么长期推进 IPv6 对实现可扩展性至关重要，并讨论对网络架构师与服务提供商的实际影响。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/28/top_stories_10282026'><span class='en'>Oct 28</span><span class='zh'>10月28日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>300</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50287775' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>416</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50306442" data-id="50306442" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Programming|Hardware|Gaming|Code" data-score="280" data-comments="141" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50306442" data-time="1793378422">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dbreunig/nesformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A working Nintendo in ~1GB of Qwen weights</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在约 1GB 的 Qwen 权重中运行一台可工作的任天堂主机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project compiles an NES CPU into the weights of a standard Qwen3.5 model.</span><span class='zh'>该项目将 NES CPU 编译进标准 Qwen3.5 模型的权重中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project compiles an NES CPU into the weights of a standard Qwen3.5 model. The NES 6502 CPU, 2 KB of work RAM, and cartridge ROM run inside the model checkpoint, with every weight computed by a compiler rather than learned through training. During play, the model predicts the CPU execution log token by token while a host program handles graphics, controls, and the vertical-blank interrupt.</span><span class='zh'>该项目将 NES CPU 编译进标准 Qwen3.5 模型的权重中。NES 的 6502 CPU、2 KB 工作内存和卡带程序 ROM 都运行在这个模型检查点内，所有权重均由编译器计算得出，而不是通过训练学习。游戏运行时，模型逐 token 预测 CPU 执行日志，主机程序则负责绘图、读取手柄输入和触发垂直消隐中断。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/auto-ontology" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia – open-source auto-ontology</span><span class='zh'>Nvidia：开源自动本体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Auto Ontology is an open Python framework for building semantic, graph-based reasoning layers over data.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Auto Ontology 是一个用于构建语义化图推理层的开源 Python 框架。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Auto Ontology is an open Python framework for building semantic, graph-based reasoning layers over data. It reconciles enterprise metadata and definitions across systems, helping agents locate, interpret, and retrieve data more reliably. The layer is exposed to agent systems through MCP, CLI, and API, and supports text-to-SQL evaluation workflows.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Auto Ontology 是一个用于构建语义化图推理层的开源 Python 框架。它整合企业元数据并协调不同系统中的定义，帮助智能体更可靠地定位、理解和检索数据。该推理层可通过 MCP、CLI 和 API 接入智能体，也支持 text-to-SQL 评测流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/herdrdev/herdr-app" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Herdr has released native app</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 发布原生应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Herdr released a native app, expanding beyond its earlier web or cross-platform offering and delivering faster performance, offline support, and deeper OS integration.</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 发布了原生应用，扩展了其此前的网页/跨平台版本，带来更快的性能、离线支持和更深的系统集成。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Herdr released a native app, expanding beyond its earlier web or cross-platform offering and delivering faster performance, offline support, and deeper OS integration. The move signals a maturation in the product and potential for broader platform reach.</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 发布了原生应用，扩展了其此前的网页/跨平台版本，带来更快的性能、离线支持和更深的系统集成。此举标志着产品成熟度提升，并可能带来更广泛的应用平台覆盖。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/da-luce/astroterm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Astroterm: A Planetarium for Your Terminal</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm：让你的终端成为行星仪</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astroterm is a terminal-based planetarium that renders sky maps and astronomical data directly in your shell.</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm 将星空投影带入终端，直接在外壳中渲染天图与星历数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astroterm is a terminal-based planetarium that renders sky maps and astronomical data directly in your shell. It brings rich visualization to environments without a full GUI, making it a handy tool for quick stargazing and education.</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm 将星空投影带入终端，直接在外壳中渲染天图与星历数据。即使在无完整图形界面的环境下，也能进行直观的天文可视化，适合快速观星与学习。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SheepSeb/tinyworldformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A tiny world model running games on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上运行的微型世界模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A tiny world model runs on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W, showing that edge AI is creeping into microcontrollers.</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上跑出一个极小的世界模型，展示边缘AI在微控制器上的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A tiny world model runs on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W, showing that edge AI is creeping into microcontrollers. The demo demonstrates memory-efficient planning and small neural models that fit in ultra‑compact hardware, hinting at future autonomous nodes in IoT. It signals potential for smarter, low-power devices at the edge.</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上跑出一个极小的世界模型，展示边缘AI在微控制器上的潜力。该演示表明在资源受限设备上也能运行简短的神经模型和规划任务。预示着物联网边缘设备将拥有更智能的能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/raphlinus/nibnib" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nibnib: Image compression with extreme decompression speed</span><span class='zh'>Nibnib：解压速度极快的图像压缩格式</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nibnib is an image format using 4 bits per pixel and optimized for extremely fast decompression.</span><span class='zh'>Nibnib 是一种每像素 4 bit、针对极快解压速度优化的图像格式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nibnib is an image format using 4 bits per pixel and optimized for extremely fast decompression. It began as an experiment targeting DVI output on an RP2350 microcontroller, where roughly six cycles per pixel must cover pixel fetching, palette lookup, and storage in a line buffer.</span><span class='zh'>Nibnib 是一种每像素 4 bit、针对极快解压速度优化的图像格式。它最初是为 RP2350 微控制器上的 DVI 输出进行的实验，该设备每个像素约有 6 个时钟周期，需要完成像素读取、调色板查询和写入行缓冲区。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>186</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50299811' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>32</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50298964" data-id="50298964" data-tags="Open Source|Security|Web|Code" data-score="166" data-comments="77" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50298964" data-time="1793325524">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/picocss/pico/discussions/754" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pico CSS is no longer maintained</span><span class='zh'>Pico CSS 不再维护</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>With official maintenance ended, users should plan forks, migrations, or look for alternatives to keep security and compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>官方宣布不再维护，用户需要考虑分叉、迁移或替代方案以维持安全性与兼容性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>With official maintenance ended, users should plan forks, migrations, or look for alternatives to keep security and compatibility. The post may offer guidance on how to migrate or handle breaking changes while preserving the lightweight, accessible styling Pico CSS is known for.</span><span class='zh'>官方宣布不再维护，用户需要考虑分叉、迁移或替代方案以维持安全性与兼容性。文中给出迁移建议与应对潜在改动的途径，同时保留 Pico CSS 的简洁与可访问性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Auto Dismiss Mac OS &#x27;Allow app to find devices on local networks?&#x27; is a small open-source tool that automatically closes that permission prompt.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 项目自动拒绝 macOS 的“允许应用发现本地网络设备？</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Auto Dismiss Mac OS &#x27;Allow app to find devices on local networks?&#x27; is a small open-source tool that automatically closes that permission prompt. It highlights the tension between a smooth UX and strict local-network permissions, inviting a discussion on safety vs convenience. The project serves as a reminder of how permission prompts influence everyday security decisions.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 项目自动拒绝 macOS 的“允许应用发现本地网络设备？”提示，提升了使用体验。产品背后的讨论聚焦于 UX 与本地网络权限之间的权衡，以及安全治理的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kerlenton/bootgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A language model that lives in a boot sector</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：运行在引导扇区中的语言模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BootGPT places an entire inference engine in a 510-byte MBR, with model weights, vocabulary, and strings stored in the following disk sectors.</span><span class='zh'>BootGPT 将完整推理引擎放入 510 字节的 MBR 中，并把模型权重、词汇表和字符串存储在后续磁盘扇区。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BootGPT places an entire inference engine in a 510-byte MBR, with model weights, vocabulary, and strings stored in the following disk sectors. Its small character-level neural network was trained to generate Linux boot messages indefinitely, matching a simulator character by character. Debugging uncovered an x86 assembly bug involving the remainder left in EDX by integer division and a pointer that also used EDX.</span><span class='zh'>BootGPT 将完整推理引擎放入 510 字节的 MBR 中，并把模型权重、词汇表和字符串存储在后续磁盘扇区。这个小型字符级神经网络经过训练，可以无限生成 Linux 启动消息，并与模拟器逐字符匹配。调试过程中发现了一个 x86 汇编错误：整数除法会把余数留在 EDX，而偏置表指针也使用了 EDX。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>105</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315782' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>6</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50297697" data-id="50297697" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Code" data-score="99" data-comments="31" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50297697" data-time="1793316346">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mottopanikeiku/doomformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I Turned DOOM into a Transformer</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：把 DOOM 变成了 Transformer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Using DOOM as a testbed, the project applies Transformer-based models to game data, demonstrating how sequence models can learn planning or perception tasks in a classic game.</span><span class='zh'>在 DOOM 的数据上应用 Transformer，展示序列模型在经典游戏中的学习能力，涉及规划与感知任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Using DOOM as a testbed, the project applies Transformer-based models to game data, demonstrating how sequence models can learn planning or perception tasks in a classic game. It’s a compact, proof-of-concept that sparks ideas about cross-domain AI experiments.</span><span class='zh'>在 DOOM 的数据上应用 Transformer，展示序列模型在经典游戏中的学习能力，涉及规划与感知任务。这个小型的实现是跨领域 AI 实验的一个有趣原型，值得关注。</span></p>
